                        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
                          const passwordInputTrigger = document.querySelector('#password_input_trigger');
                          const passwordInput = document.querySelector('#password_input');
                          passwordInputTrigger.onclick = () => {
                            if (passwordInput.type === 'password') {
                              passwordInput.type = 'text';
                              return;
                            }
                            passwordInput.type = 'password';

                                    <div class="mask">
                                       <ul class="announcement-list newsticker" id="announcement_list" style="width: 22359px; left: -429.48px; font-family: 'Arial', sans-serif; font-size: 14px;">
                                          <li style="font-weight: 500;"> 
                                             <strong style="color: #ff0000;">HOT NEWS:</strong> <span style="text-transform: uppercase; font-weight: 700;">Slot Gacor</span> merilis bocoran pola <span style="color: #008000;">anti rungkad</span> terbaru hari ini untuk membantu slotter Indonesia meraih maxwin kilat di agen terpercaya.

      <script>
         // Fungsi untuk mendapatkan nama hari
         function getDayName(dayIndex) {
           const days = ["Min", "Sen", "Sel", "Rab", "Kam", "Jum", "Sab"];
           return days[dayIndex] || "";
         }
         // Dapatkan elemen tanggal
         const dateElement = document.querySelector('[data-section="date"]');
         if (dateElement) {
           const today = new Date();
           const dayName = getDayName(today.getDay());
           const dd = String(today.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
           const mm = String(today.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"); // bulan dimulai dari 0
           const yy = String(today.getFullYear()).slice(-2); // dua digit tahun
           dateElement.textContent = `${dayName}, ${dd}/${mm}/${yy} - Powered by Slot Gacor`;
         }
      </script>
